  /* ---------- 请求 ---------- */
  function apiGet(path, cb) {
    var $ = window.jQuery || window.$;
    if ($ && $.ajax) {
      $.ajax({
        url: API + path, type: 'GET', dataType: 'json',
        xhrFields: { withCredentials: true }, timeout: 20000,
        success: function (d) { cb(d); },
        error: function () { cb(null); }
      });
      return;
    }
    fetch(API + path, { credentials: 'include' })
      .then(function (r) { return r.json(); })
      .then(cb).catch(function () { cb(null); });
  }
  function apiPost(path, body, cb) {
    var $ = window.jQuery || window.$;
    if ($ && $.ajax) {
      $.ajax({
        url: API + path, type: 'POST', dataType: 'json',
        contentType: 'application/json',
        data: JSON.stringify(body == null ? null : body),   // 与旧组件 postData 一致：null 也发成字符串 "null"
        xhrFields: { withCredentials: true }, timeout: 20000,
        success: function (d) { cb(d); },
        error: function () { cb(null); }
      });
      return;
    }
    fetch(API + path, {
      method: 'POST', credentials: 'include',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(body == null ? null : body)
    }).then(function (r) { return r.json(); }).then(cb).catch(function () { cb(null); });
  }

  /* 全量翻页（按 CharacterId 去重） */
  function fetchAll(makePath, cb) {
    var out = [], seen = {}, page = 1, CAP = 20;
    function step() {
      apiGet(makePath(page), function (d) {
        var v = (d && d.State === 0) ? d.Value : null;
        if (!v || !v.Items) { cb(out, page > 1); return; }
        for (var i = 0; i < v.Items.length; i++) {
          var it = v.Items[i] || {};
          var id = String(it.CharacterId != null ? it.CharacterId : it.Id);
          if (!id || seen[id]) continue;
          seen[id] = 1;
          out.push(it);
        }
        var tp = Number(v.TotalPages) || 1;
        if (page < tp && page < CAP) { page++; step(); return; }
        cb(out, true);
      });
    }
    step();
  }

  /* 部分六是独立 IIFE，取不到部分五里的 charaAvatarHtml —— 之前直接调用会抛
     ReferenceError，renderList 中断，面板停在「正在加载英灵殿数据…」。这里自带一份。 */
  /* 英灵殿头像失败兜底（与部分五同一套逻辑，各自作用域各绑一次） */
  if (!window.__tgsAvaErrBound) {
    window.__tgsAvaErrBound = true;
    document.addEventListener('error', function (e) {
      var t = e.target;
      if (!t || t.tagName !== 'IMG') return;
      /* 先换备用地址（去尺寸后缀 / bgm 默认头像），全试完才退化成占位块 */
      var alt = t.getAttribute ? t.getAttribute('data-tgs-alt') : '';
      if (alt) {
        var alts = String(alt).split('|');
        var nxt = alts.shift();
        t.setAttribute('data-tgs-alt', alts.join('|'));
        if (nxt) { try { t.setAttribute('src', nxt); } catch (eA) { } return; }
      }
      if (!t.classList || !t.classList.contains('tgs-ava')) return;
      t.classList.add('tgs-ava-empty');
      try { t.removeAttribute('src'); } catch (err) { }
    }, true);
  }

  function vhAvatar(it) {
    if (!it) return '<span class="tgs-ava vh-ava tgs-ava-empty"></span>';
    var raw = it.Icon || it.Cover || it.Avatar || it.Image || '';
    if (!raw) return '<span class="tgs-ava vh-ava tgs-ava-empty"></span>';
    var u = String(raw);
    if (u.indexOf('https://tinygrail.oss-cn-hangzhou.aliyuncs.com/') === 0) {
      u = 'https://tinygrail.mange.cn' + u.substr(46) + '!w240';
    } else if (u.indexOf('/avatar') === 0) {
      u = 'https://tinygrail.mange.cn' + u + '!w240';
    } else {
      u = u.replace('http://', '//');
    }
    return '<img class="tgs-ava vh-ava" loading="lazy" decoding="async" src="' + u + '" alt="">';
  }

  function num(v) { var n = Number(v); return isNaN(n) ? 0 : n; }
  function fmt(n) {
    n = Math.round(Number(n) || 0);
    return String(n).replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ',');
  }
  function money(v) {
    var n = Number(v);
    return isNaN(n) ? '—' : '₵' + n.toFixed(2).replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ',');
  }
  function escHtml(s) {
    return String(s || '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
      return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c];
    });
  }
  function username(cb) {
    apiGet('chara/user/assets', function (d) {
      var v = (d && d.State === 0) ? d.Value : null;
      cb(v ? (v.Name || '') : '');
    });
  }

  /* ---------- 数据 ---------- */
  function loadAll(cb) {
    if (data.all && data.hold && data.temple) { cb(); return; }
    if (data.loading) { data.waits = data.waits || []; data.waits.push(cb); return; }
    data.loading = true;
    data.err = '';
    data.waits = [cb];
    var pending = 3, fails = 0;   // valhalla + holdings + temples 三个回调（原来写成 4，首次打开永远不回调）
    function done() {
      if (--pending > 0) return;
      data.loading = false;
      var ws = data.waits || [];
      data.waits = [];
      for (var i = 0; i < ws.length; i++) ws[i]();
    }
    function fail() { fails++; done(); }

    fetchAll(function (p) { return 'chara/user/chara/tinygrail/' + p + '/500'; }, function (list, ok) {
      if (!ok) { data.err = '英灵殿列表加载失败'; fail(); return; }
      data.all = list;
      done();
    });

    username(function (name) {
      /* 收藏夹按账号分开存（同一个浏览器可能登过不同账号）；
         用户名拿到就先把收藏读出来，列表渲染时星形状态才是对的。 */
      if (name && name !== vhFavUser) vhFavLoad(name);
      if (!name) { data.err = '拿不到当前用户名（登录状态异常？）'; fail(); fail(); return; }
      fetchAll(function (p) { return 'chara/user/chara/' + encodeURIComponent(name) + '/' + p + '/500'; }, function (h, ok) {
        var map = {};
        for (var i = 0; i < h.length; i++) {
          var id = String(h[i].CharacterId != null ? h[i].CharacterId : h[i].Id);
          if (num(h[i].UserAmount) > 0) map[id] = 1;
        }
        data.hold = map;
        done();
      });
      /* chara/user/temple 需要带 keyword 参数（旧组件就是这么调的），
         每页条数也不一定吃 500 → 依次退档重试，避免整列取不到数据 */
      function takeTemples(t) {
        var map = {};
        for (var i = 0; i < (t || []).length; i++) {
          var it = t[i] || {};
          var id = String(it.CharacterId != null ? it.CharacterId : it.Id);
          var s = num(it.Sacrifices);
          if (s > 0) map[id] = { sac: s, assets: num(it.Assets), lv: num(it.Level) };
        }
        return map;
      }
      /* 只试 500 一档；空就当成「我没有圣殿」，不再换尺寸重跑全量翻页
         （3 档都试一遍等于 3 倍请求量，对没建塔的用户是纯浪费）。
         真拿不到数据时底部会提示，用户点刷新即可。 */
      fetchAll(function (p) {
        return 'chara/user/temple/' + encodeURIComponent(name) + '/' + p + '/500?keyword=';
      }, function (t) {
        var map = takeTemples(t);
        data.temple = map;
        if (!Object.keys(map).length) data.templeWarn = true;
        done();
      });
    });
  }

  /* 竞拍人数 / 数量：POST 一批 id */
  function loadAuctions(ids, cb, force) {
    /* 只请求还没取过竞拍信息的角色：翻页来回切不会重复打接口。
       出价成功后传 force=true 强刷那一只。 */
    var need = [];
    for (var q = 0; q < ids.length; q++) {
      var k0 = String(ids[q]);
      if (force || !data.auc[k0]) need.push(ids[q]);
    }
    if (!need.length) { cb(); return; }
    var batch = need.slice(0, 200);
    apiPost('chara/auction/list', batch, function (d) {
      var v = (d && d.State === 0) ? d.Value : null;
      if (Object.prototype.toString.call(v) === '[object Array]') {
        for (var i = 0; i < v.length; i++) {
          var a = v[i] || {};
          var id = String(a.CharacterId != null ? a.CharacterId : '');
          if (!id) continue;
          data.auc[id] = { n: num(a.State), qty: num(a.Type), price: num(a.Price), amount: num(a.Amount) };
        }
      }
      cb();
    });
  }

  /* ---------- 筛选 / 排序 ---------- */
  function filtered() {
    var src = data.all || [];
    var hold = data.hold || {}, temple = data.temple || {};
    var out = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < src.length; i++) {
      var it = src[i];
      var id = String(it.CharacterId != null ? it.CharacterId : it.Id);
      var lv = num(it.Level);
      if (f.lv > 0 && lv !== f.lv) continue;
      if (f.holdOnly && !hold[id]) continue;
      /* 仅看收藏：只留点过星形的股（股没了 → 它根本不在 data.all 里，
         由 renderList 给出「收藏的股已不在英灵殿」的说明） */
      if (f.favOnly && !vhFavHas(id)) continue;
      if (f.noTemple) {
        /* 只看我自己：我在这个角色名下的献祭值 < 500 才算「未建塔」。
           别人建的塔不影响判断。 */
        var myT = temple[id];
        if (myT && num(myT.sac) >= TEMPLE_LINE) continue;
      }
      if (f.kw) {
        var kw = f.kw.toLowerCase();
        if (String(id).indexOf(kw) < 0 && String(it.Name || '').toLowerCase().indexOf(kw) < 0) continue;
      }
      it.__id = id;
      out.push(it);
    }
    /* 英灵殿内可竞拍股数（各接口字段名不一，按 UserTotal / UserAmount / State 兜底） */
  function vhShares(it) {
    if (!it) return 0;
    if (it.UserTotal != null) return num(it.UserTotal);
    if (it.UserAmount != null) return num(it.UserAmount);
    return num(it.State);
  }

  /* 默认：英灵殿内可竞拍股数从高到低（量大的在前面更好挑）；
       勾了「价格从高到低」则按当前价格排 */
    if (f.byPrice) {
      out.sort(function (a, b) {
        var d = num(b.Current) - num(a.Current);
        return d || (vhShares(b) - vhShares(a));
      });
    } else {
      out.sort(function (a, b) {
        var d = vhShares(b) - vhShares(a);
        return d || (num(a.CharacterId || a.Id) - num(b.CharacterId || b.Id));
      });
    }
    return out;
  }

  /* ---------- 渲染 ---------- */
  var pop = null;

  function levelCounts() {
    var src = data.all || [];
    var c = {};
    for (var i = 0; i < src.length; i++) {
      var lv = num(src[i].Level);
      c[lv] = (c[lv] || 0) + 1;
    }
    return c;
  }

  function render() {
    if (!pop) return;
    var counts = levelCounts();
    var chips = '<span class="vh-chip' + (f.lv === 0 ? ' on' : '') + '" data-lv="0">全部 <i>' + (data.all ? data.all.length : 0) + '</i></span>';
    Object.keys(counts).map(Number).sort(function (a, b) { return a - b; }).forEach(function (lv) {
      if (lv <= 0) return;
      chips += '<span class="vh-chip' + (f.lv === lv ? ' on' : '') + '" data-lv="' + lv + '">Lv' + lv +
        ' <i>' + counts[lv] + '</i></span>';
    });
    chips += '<span class="vh-chip' + (f.holdOnly ? ' on' : '') + '" data-flag="holdOnly">仅看有持股</span>';
    chips += '<span class="vh-chip' + (f.noTemple ? ' on' : '') + '" data-flag="noTemple">未建塔</span>';
    chips += '<span class="vh-chip vh-chip-fav' + (f.favOnly ? ' on' : '') + '" data-flag="favOnly" title="只看你点过星形的股（收藏夹）">仅看收藏 <i>' + fmt(vhFavCount()) + '</i></span>';
    chips += '<span class="vh-chip' + (f.byPrice ? ' on' : '') + '" data-flag="byPrice">价格从高到低</span>';

    var filt = pop.querySelector('.vh-filters');
    var oldSearch = pop.querySelector('.vh-search');
    var kw = oldSearch ? oldSearch.value : f.kw;
    filt.innerHTML = chips +
      '<input class="vh-search" placeholder="搜角色名 / 编号" value="' + escHtml(kw) + '">';
    var se = filt.querySelector('.vh-search');
    if (se) {
      se.addEventListener('input', function () {
        f.kw = se.value.trim();
        f.page = 1;
        renderList();
      });
    }
    filt.querySelectorAll('.vh-chip').forEach(function (chip) {
      chip.addEventListener('click', function () {
        var lv = chip.getAttribute('data-lv');
        var flag = chip.getAttribute('data-flag');
        if (lv != null) f.lv = parseInt(lv, 10) || 0;
        else if (flag === 'holdOnly') f.holdOnly = !f.holdOnly;
        else if (flag === 'noTemple') f.noTemple = !f.noTemple;
        else if (flag === 'favOnly') f.favOnly = !f.favOnly;
        else if (flag === 'byPrice') f.byPrice = !f.byPrice;
        f.page = 1;
        render();
      });
    });
    renderList();
  }

  /* 底部提示：有收藏的股已经不在英灵殿（退市 / 拍完）时提醒一句 ——
     用户要的「英灵殿没股了也要给出没股的信息」，正常浏览列表时也能看到。 */
  function favGoneNote() {
    var n = vhFavCount();
    if (!n) return '';
    var seen = {}, src = data.all || [], i;
    for (i = 0; i < src.length; i++) seen[String(src[i].CharacterId != null ? src[i].CharacterId : src[i].Id)] = 1;
    var gone = 0, k;
    for (k in vhFavSet) if (vhFavSet[k] && !seen[k]) gone++;
    if (!gone) return '';
    return '<span style="color:#b8860b">· 收藏里有 ' + fmt(gone) + ' 只股已不在英灵殿（无股）</span>';
  }

  /* 「仅看收藏」筛不出东西时的说明 —— 用户要的「股没了也要给出没股的信息」：
     ① 有收藏、但收藏的股现在都不在英灵殿（退市 / 被拍完）→ 逐条列出哪几只没股了
     ② 一只都没收藏 → 提示怎么收藏 */
  function favEmptyHtml() {
    var extra = (f.holdOnly ? '（叠加了「仅看有持股」）' : '') +
      (f.noTemple ? '（叠加了「未建塔」）' : '') +
      (f.kw ? '（叠加了搜索「' + escHtml(f.kw) + '」）' : '');
    if (f.favOnly) {
      var favIds = [], k;
      for (k in vhFavSet) if (vhFavSet[k]) favIds.push(k);
      if (!favIds.length) {
        return '<div class="vh-favdone">收藏夹是空的 —— 在列表里点每行<b>序号左边的星形</b>即可收藏。<br>' +
          '收藏会记住，下次打开仍在。</div>';
      }
      var gone = [], still = 0, seen = {};
      var src = data.all || [];
      for (var i = 0; i < src.length; i++) {
        var sid = String(src[i].CharacterId != null ? src[i].CharacterId : src[i].Id);
        seen[sid] = 1;
      }
      /* 收藏里「已不在英灵殿列表」的（退市 / 拍完下架）—— 股没了也要告诉用户 */
      for (var j = 0; j < favIds.length; j++) if (!seen[favIds[j]]) gone.push(favIds[j]);
      still = favIds.length - gone.length;
      if (!still && gone.length) {
        return '<div class="vh-favdone">收藏的 <b>' + fmt(gone.length) + '</b> 只股<b>现在英灵殿里已经没有股了</b>' +
          (extra ? '<br><span style="font-size:11px">' + extra + '</span>' : '') +
          '<br><span style="font-size:11px;color:#aaa">收藏仍然保留，等它们回到英灵殿就会重新出现。</span>' +
          '<div style="margin-top:6px;font-size:11px;color:#888;word-break:break-all">#' + gone.join(' · #') + '</div></div>';
      }
      return '<div class="vh-favdone">收藏的股<b>没有符合当前筛选条件</b>的' +
        (extra ? '<br><span style="font-size:11px">' + extra + '</span>' : '') +
        (gone.length ? '<br><span style="font-size:11px;color:#aaa">另有 ' + fmt(gone.length) + ' 只收藏的股已不在英灵殿</span>' : '') +
        '</div>';
    }
    return '<div class="vh-empty">没有符合筛选条件的股票</div>';
  }

  function renderList() {
    if (!pop) return;
    var box = pop.querySelector('.vh-list');
    var foot = pop.querySelector('.vh-foot');
    if (!data.all) {
      box.innerHTML = '<div class="vh-empty">' + (data.err ? escHtml(data.err) : '正在加载英灵殿数据…') + '</div>';
      foot.innerHTML = '';
      return;
    }
    var list = filtered();
    var total = list.length;
    var pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / PAGE_SIZE));
    if (f.page > pages) f.page = pages;
    var start = (f.page - 1) * PAGE_SIZE;
    var slice = list.slice(start, start + PAGE_SIZE);



    if (!total) {
      box.innerHTML = favEmptyHtml();
    } else {
      var hold = data.hold || {}, temple = data.temple || {};
      var html = '';
      for (var i = 0; i < slice.length; i++) {
        var it = slice[i];
        var id = it.__id || String(it.CharacterId != null ? it.CharacterId : it.Id);
        var lv = num(it.Level);
        var price = num(it.Price);
        var shares = num(it.UserTotal != null ? it.UserTotal : (it.UserAmount != null ? it.UserAmount : it.State));
        var a = data.auc[id] || { n: 0, qty: 0 };
        var hasTemple = !!(temple[id] && num(temple[id].sac) >= TEMPLE_LINE);
        html += '<div class="vh-row">' +
          '<span class="vh-fav' + (vhFavHas(id) ? ' on' : '') + '" data-fav="' + id + '" title="收藏 / 取消收藏（在「仅看收藏」里只看点过星形的股）">' +
            (vhFavHas(id) ? '★' : '☆') + '</span>' +
          '<span class="vh-rank">' + (start + i + 1) + '</span>' +
          vhAvatar(it) +
          '<span class="vh-lv' + (lv ? ' lv' + lv : '') + '">Lv' + lv + '</span>' +
          '<span class="vh-name">#' + id + ' ' +
            '<a class="tgs-chara-link" href="/character/' + encodeURIComponent(id) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">' +
              escHtml(it.Name || ('#' + id)) + '</a>' +
            (hold[id] ? ' <span style="color:#5aa06e;font-size:10.5px">持股</span>' : '') +
            (hasTemple ? ' <span style="color:#c0605e;font-size:10.5px">已建塔</span>' : '') +
          '</span>' +
          '<span class="vh-num" title="当前价格">' + money(it.Current) + '</span>' +
          '<span class="vh-num" title="我的固定资产 / 献祭值（上限）">固 ' +
            (data.temple[id] ? (fmt(data.temple[id].assets) + '/' + fmt(data.temple[id].sac)) : '—') + '</span>' +
          '<span class="vh-num" title="拍卖底价 / 可竞拍股数">底 <b>' + money(price) + '</b> / ' + fmt(shares) + ' 股</span>' +
          '<span class="vh-num" title="竞拍人数 / 竞拍数量">竞 ' + fmt(a.n) + ' / ' + fmt(a.qty) + '</span>' +
          '<span class="vh-act" data-bid="' + id + '">出价</span>' +
          '<span class="vh-act hist" data-hist="' + id + '">往期</span>' +
          '</div>';
      }
      box.innerHTML = html;
    }

    foot.innerHTML = '共 <b>' + fmt(total) + '</b> 只 · 第 ' + f.page + ' / ' + pages + ' 页' +
      (data.templeWarn ? '<span style="color:#c0605e">· 我的圣殿数据没取到，固定资产列为空</span>' : '') +
      favGoneNote() +
      '<span class="vh-pg' + (f.page <= 1 ? ' off' : '') + '" data-pg="-1">上一页</span>' +
      '<span class="vh-pg' + (f.page >= pages ? ' off' : '') + '" data-pg="1">下一页</span>';

    foot.querySelectorAll('.vh-pg').forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener('click', function () {
        var d = parseInt(b.getAttribute('data-pg'), 10) || 0;
        f.page = Math.max(1, Math.min(pages, f.page + d));
        renderList();
      });
    });
    box.querySelectorAll('[data-bid]').forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener('click', function () { openBid(b.getAttribute('data-bid')); });
    });
    box.querySelectorAll('[data-hist]').forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener('click', function () { openHist(b.getAttribute('data-hist')); });
    });
    /* 星形：点亮 / 熄灭收藏。就地改这一行 + 顶部的「仅看收藏」计数，
       不整表重绘（否则在长列表里点一下会跳回顶部）。
       若正开在「仅看收藏」里取消收藏 → 这一行该消失，重绘一次列表。 */
    box.querySelectorAll('[data-fav]').forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener('click', function () {
        var id = b.getAttribute('data-fav');
        var on = vhFavToggle(id);
        b.classList.toggle('on', on);
        b.textContent = on ? '★' : '☆';
        var chipC = pop && pop.querySelector('.vh-chip-fav i');
        if (chipC) chipC.textContent = fmt(vhFavCount());
        if (f.favOnly && !on) renderList();
      });
    });
  }

  /* ---------- 出价 ---------- */
  function openBid(id) {
    var it = null;
    for (var i = 0; i < (data.all || []).length; i++) {
      var x = data.all[i];
      if (String(x.CharacterId != null ? x.CharacterId : x.Id) === String(id)) { it = x; break; }
    }
    if (!it) return;
    closeEl('tgs-vhbid');
    var price = num(it.Price);
    var shares = num(it.UserTotal != null ? it.UserTotal : (it.UserAmount != null ? it.UserAmount : it.State));
    var box = document.createElement('div');
    box.id = 'tgs-vhbid';
    box.innerHTML =
      '<div class="hb-head"><span>竞拍 #' + id + '「' + escHtml(it.Name || '') + '」</span>' +
        '<span class="vh-close" data-close="1">×</span></div>' +
      '<div class="hb-body">' +
        '<div class="hb-line"><span>出价</span><input type="number" min="0" step="0.01" class="bid-price" value="' + price + '"></div>' +
        '<div class="hb-line"><span>数量</span><input type="number" min="1" step="1" class="bid-amount" value="' + shares + '"></div>' +
        '<div style="color:#999;font-size:11px">底价 ' + money(price) + ' · 可竞拍 ' + fmt(shares) + ' 股</div>' +
      '</div>' +
      '<div class="hb-foot"><div class="hb-btn cancel" data-close="1">取消</div><div class="hb-btn ok">确认出价</div></div>';
    document.body.appendChild(box);
    box.querySelectorAll('[data-close]').forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener('click', function () { closeEl('tgs-vhbid'); });
    });
    box.querySelector('.hb-btn.ok').addEventListener('click', function () {
      var p = num(box.querySelector('.bid-price').value);
      var a = num(box.querySelector('.bid-amount').value);
      if (!(p > 0) || !(a > 0)) { alert('出价和数量都要大于 0'); return; }
      this.textContent = '提交中…';
      var self = this;
      apiPost('chara/auction/' + id + '/' + p + '/' + a, null, function (d) {
        if (d && d.State === 0) {
          closeEl('tgs-vhbid');
          loadAuctions([String(id)], function () { renderList(); }, true);   // 出价成功 → 强刷这一只
        } else {
          self.textContent = '确认出价';
          alert('出价失败：' + ((d && (d.Message || d.Error)) || '接口无返回'));
        }
      });
    });
  }

  /* ---------- 往期 ---------- */
  function openHist(id) {
    closeEl('tgs-vhhist');
    var box = document.createElement('div');
    box.id = 'tgs-vhhist';
    box.innerHTML = '<div class="hb-head"><span>往期拍卖 · #' + id + '</span>' +
      '<span class="vh-close" data-close="1">×</span></div>' +
      '<div class="hb-list"><div class="vh-empty">加载中…</div></div>';
    document.body.appendChild(box);
    box.querySelector('[data-close]').addEventListener('click', function () { closeEl('tgs-vhhist'); });
    apiGet('chara/auction/list/' + id + '/1', function (d) {
      var v = (d && d.State === 0) ? d.Value : null;
      var list = box.querySelector('.hb-list');
      if (Object.prototype.toString.call(v) !== '[object Array]' || !v.length) {
        list.innerHTML = '<div class="vh-empty">暂无拍卖记录</div>';
        return;
      }
      var ok = 0, qty = 0, html = '';
      for (var i = 0; i < v.length; i++) {
        var a = v[i] || {};
        var win = num(a.State) === 1;
        if (win) { ok++; qty += num(a.Amount); }
        var t = String(a.Bid || '').replace('T', ' ').slice(0, 16);
        html += '<div class="hb-row">' +
          '<span class="hb-time">' + escHtml(t) + '</span>' +
          '<span class="hb-user">' + escHtml(a.Nickname || a.Username || '') + '</span>' +
          '<span class="hb-price">' + money(a.Price) + ' / ' + fmt(a.Amount) + '</span>' +
          '<span class="hb-tag ' + (win ? 'ok' : 'no') + '">' + (win ? '成功' : '失败') + '</span>' +
          '</div>';
      }
      list.innerHTML = '<div style="padding:4px 2px 8px;color:#888">共 ' + v.length + ' 人参与，成功 ' +
        ok + ' 人 / ' + fmt(qty) + ' 股</div>' + html;
    });
  }

  function closeEl(id) {
    var el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el && el.parentNode) el.parentNode.removeChild(el);
  }

  /* ---------- 面板 ---------- */
  /* 英灵殿窗口位置：拖动后记住，关掉再打开还在原处；刷新页面回到居中 */
  var vhPos = null;

  function makeVhDraggable() {
    var head = pop && pop.querySelector('.vh-head');
    if (!head || head.__tgcDrag) return;
    head.__tgcDrag = true;
    head.style.cursor = 'move';
    var drag = false, sx = 0, sy = 0, ox = 0, oy = 0;
    function onMove(e) {
      if (!drag) return;
      var nx = ox + (e.clientX - sx), ny = oy + (e.clientY - sy);
      nx = Math.max(2, Math.min(window.innerWidth - pop.offsetWidth - 2, nx));
      ny = Math.max(2, Math.min(window.innerHeight - 60, ny));
      pop.style.left = nx + 'px';
      pop.style.top = ny + 'px';
      e.preventDefault();
    }
    function onUp() {
      if (!drag) return;
      drag = false;
      vhPos = { x: pop.offsetLeft, y: pop.offsetTop };
      document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
      document.removeEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
    }
    head.addEventListener('mousedown', function (e) {
      if (e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.vh-close')) return;
      var r = pop.getBoundingClientRect();
      drag = true;
      sx = e.clientX; sy = e.clientY; ox = r.left; oy = r.top;
      pop.style.transform = 'none';
      pop.style.left = r.left + 'px';
      pop.style.top = r.top + 'px';
      document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove);
      document.addEventListener('mouseup', onUp);
      e.preventDefault();
    });
  }

  function openPanel() {
    if (pop) return;
    pop = document.createElement('div');
    pop.id = 'tgs-vhpop';
    pop.innerHTML =
      '<div class="vh-head"><span>英灵殿 · 全部股票</span><span class="vh-close">×</span></div>' +
      '<div class="vh-filters"></div>' +
      '<div class="vh-list"><div class="vh-empty">正在加载英灵殿数据…</div></div>' +
      '<div class="vh-foot"></div>';
    document.body.appendChild(pop);
    pop.querySelector('.vh-close').addEventListener('click', closePanel);
    if (vhPos) {
      pop.style.transform = 'none';
      pop.style.left = Math.max(2, Math.min(window.innerWidth - 100, vhPos.x)) + 'px';
      pop.style.top = Math.max(2, Math.min(window.innerHeight - 60, vhPos.y)) + 'px';
    }
    makeVhDraggable();
    render();
    loadAll(function () {
      if (!pop) return;
      if (data.err) { renderList(); return; }
      /* 拉完列表后补齐当前页的竞拍人数 / 数量 */
      var ids = filtered().slice(0, 200).map(function (it) {
        return String(it.CharacterId != null ? it.CharacterId : it.Id);
      });
      loadAuctions(ids, function () { if (pop) renderList(); });
      render();
    });
  }
  function closePanel() {
    closeEl('tgs-vhpop');
    pop = null;
  }

  /* ---------- 按钮 ---------- */
  function toggleVh() { if (pop) closePanel(); else openPanel(); }

  var mountTries = 0;
  function mountButton() {
    /* 并入「圣殿」悬浮窗：不再单独飘一个英灵殿按钮 */
    var panel = document.getElementById('tg-panel');
    /* 新版圣殿面板默认是懒创建的（要点右侧竖排「圣殿」标签才 buildPanel），
       这里主动点一下标签把面板建出来，否则永远等不到 → 只能退回浮动按钮 */
    if (!panel) {
      var lg = document.getElementById('tg-launcher');
      if (lg && !lg.__tgsAutoOpened) {
        lg.__tgsAutoOpened = true;
        try { lg.click(); } catch (e) { }
        panel = document.getElementById('tg-panel');
        /* 面板是「打开」状态建的 → 若原本隐藏，再点一下关掉，避免突然弹出 */
        if (panel && panel.classList.contains('tg-hidden')) { /* 已是隐藏，保持 */ }
      }
    }
    if (panel) {
      if (!document.getElementById('tgs-vh-entry')) {
        var box = document.createElement('div');
        box.id = 'tgs-vh-entry';
        box.className = 'tgs-vh-entry';
        box.innerHTML = '<span class="t">英灵殿</span>' +
          '<span class="d">全部股票 · 底价 / 竞拍 / 出价</span>';
        box.title = '打开英灵殿全部股票列表';
        box.addEventListener('click', toggleVh);
        var head = panel.querySelector('.tg-head');
        if (head && head.nextSibling) panel.insertBefore(box, head.nextSibling);
        else panel.insertBefore(box, panel.firstChild);
      }
      return;
    }
    /* 圣殿面板还没渲染好 → 再等一会儿；实在没有才退回浮动按钮 */
    if (mountTries++ < 100) { setTimeout(mountButton, 400); return; }   // 最多等 40 秒
    if (document.getElementById('tgs-vh-btn')) return;
    var b = document.createElement('div');
    b.id = 'tgs-vh-btn';
    b.textContent = '英灵殿';
    b.title = '列出英灵殿全部股票：底价 / 股数 / 竞拍人数 / 出价 / 往期';
    b.addEventListener('click', toggleVh);
    document.body.appendChild(b);
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', mountButton);
  } else {
    mountButton();
  }
})();

/* ================= 部分七：底栏开关（隐藏「圣殿」悬浮窗） =================
   在 bgm 右下角功能栏（#dock）里加一个按钮，用来显示 / 隐藏
   本组件的「圣殿」悬浮窗（#tg-launcher 竖排标签 + #tg-panel 面板）。
   状态存 localStorage，刷新后保持；开关就在底栏上，随时可以点回来。 */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (window.__tgsDockHideLoaded) return;
  window.__tgsDockHideLoaded = true;
  if (!/(^|\.)bgm\.tv$/.test(location.hostname)) return;

  var LS_KEY = 'tgs_grail_ui_hidden_v1';
  var CSS = `
html.tgs-grail-ui-off #tg-launcher,
html.tgs-grail-ui-off #tg-panel { display: none !important; }
#tgs-dock-sw {
    display: inline-flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
    width: 22px; height: 22px; border: 0; background: none; color: inherit;
    opacity: .65; cursor: pointer; padding: 0; text-decoration: none;
    transition: opacity .15s, color .15s;
}
#tgs-dock-sw:hover { opacity: 1; color: #f09199; }
#tgs-dock-sw.tgs-on { opacity: 1; color: #f09199; }
`;
  if (!document.getElementById('tgs-dock-style')) {
    var st = document.createElement('style');
    st.id = 'tgs-dock-style';
    st.textContent = CSS;
    (document.head || document.documentElement).appendChild(st);
  }

  function isHidden() {
    try { return localStorage.getItem(LS_KEY) === '1'; } catch (e) { return false; }
  }
  function apply(hidden) {
    document.documentElement.classList.toggle('tgs-grail-ui-off', !!hidden);
    try { localStorage.setItem(LS_KEY, hidden ? '1' : '0'); } catch (e) { }
    var sw = document.getElementById('tgs-dock-sw');
    if (sw) {
      sw.classList.toggle('tgs-on', !hidden);
      sw.title = hidden ? '显示「圣殿」悬浮窗' : '隐藏「圣殿」悬浮窗';
    }
  }

  function mountSwitch() {
    var dock = document.getElementById('dock');
    if (!dock) return false;
    if (document.getElementById('tgs-dock-sw')) return true;
    var ul = dock.querySelector('ul') || dock;
    var a = document.createElement('a');
    a.id = 'tgs-dock-sw';
    a.href = 'javascript:void(0);';
    a.setAttribute('role', 'button');
    a.title = isHidden() ? '显示「圣殿」悬浮窗' : '隐藏「圣殿」悬浮窗';
    a.innerHTML = '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="17" height="17" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" ' +
      'stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">' +
      '<path d="M12 3 4 8v13h16V8z"></path>' +
      '<path d="M9 21v-6h6v6"></path></svg>';
    a.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
      apply(!isHidden());
    });
    var li = document.createElement('li');
    li.className = 'tgs-dockitem';
    li.appendChild(a);
    var last = ul.querySelector('li.last') || ul.lastElementChild;
    if (last && last.parentNode === ul) ul.insertBefore(li, last);
    else ul.appendChild(li);
    a.classList.toggle('tgs-on', !isHidden());
    return true;
  }

  var tries = 0;
  function boot() {
    apply(isHidden());
    if (mountSwitch()) return;
    if (tries++ < 40) setTimeout(boot, 300);
    else apply(isHidden());
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', boot);
  } else {
    boot();
  }
})();

/* ---------- 部分六：DOM 快照诊断（独立 IIFE，不依赖上面任何模块） ----------
   用途：持股筛选认不出人物列表时，在控制台执行  __tgsDiag2()
   会打印一份「页面结构 + 最像列表的容器 + 第一张卡片的 HTML」，
   把这段整块复制发出来就能定位是哪一层结构和我猜的不一样。不修改页面。 */
(function () {
  if (window.__tgsDiag2) return;

  function clsOf(el) {
    var c = el.className;
    if (typeof c !== 'string') c = (c && c.baseVal) || '';
    c = c.replace(/^\s+|\s+$/g, '').replace(/\s+/g, '.');
    return c ? '.' + c.slice(0, 70) : '';
  }
  function idOf(el) { return el.id ? '#' + el.id : ''; }
  function cnt(el, sel) {
    try { return el.querySelectorAll(sel).length; } catch (e) { return 0; }
  }
  function vis(el) {
    try { return el.getClientRects().length > 0; } catch (e) { return false; }
  }

  window.__tgsDiag2 = function () {
    var L = [];
    var P = function (s) { L.push(s === undefined ? '' : String(s)); };

    P('URL ' + location.pathname + location.search);
    P('a[href*=/character/] ' + cnt(document, 'a[href*="/character/"]') +
      ' · img ' + cnt(document, 'img') +
      ' · #tg-temple ' + cnt(document, '#tg-temple') +
      ' · #tg-user-tinygrail ' + cnt(document, '#tg-user-tinygrail') +
      ' · #grail ' + cnt(document, '#grail') +
      ' · #tgs-tools ' + cnt(document, '#tgs-tools'));
    P('');

    var root = document.querySelector('#tg-user-tinygrail') ||
      document.querySelector('#grail') || document.body;
    P('根节点 <' + root.tagName.toLowerCase() + idOf(root) + clsOf(root) + '> 可见=' + vis(root) +
      ' 直接子节点=' + root.children.length);

    function walk(el, depth) {
      if (depth > 6) return;
      var pad = '';
      for (var i = 0; i < depth; i++) pad += '  ';
      var kids = el.children.length;
      P(pad + '<' + el.tagName.toLowerCase() + idOf(el) + clsOf(el) + '>' +
        ' kids=' + kids + ' chr=' + cnt(el, 'a[href*="/character/"]') +
        ' img=' + cnt(el, 'img') + (vis(el) ? '' : ' [隐藏]'));
      if (kids > 20) { P(pad + '  …（共 ' + kids + ' 个子节点，不再展开）'); return; }
      for (var j = 0; j < kids && j < 12; j++) walk(el.children[j], depth + 1);
    }
    walk(root, 0);

    /* 最像「重复列表」的容器：≥4 个直接子节点、且每个子节点里都有图 */
    var best = null, bestN = 0;
    var all = root.querySelectorAll('*');
    for (var i = 0; i < all.length; i++) {
      var el = all[i], k = el.children.length;
      if (k < 4) continue;
      var n = 0;
      for (var j = 0; j < k; j++) if (el.children[j].querySelector('img')) n++;
      if (n < 4 || n * 2 < k) continue;
      if (el.querySelector('#tg-temple')) continue;
      if (n > bestN) { bestN = n; best = el; }
    }
    P('');
    if (best) {
      P('最像列表的容器 <' + best.tagName.toLowerCase() + idOf(best) + clsOf(best) + '> 带图子节点 ' +
        bestN + '/' + best.children.length + ' 可见=' + vis(best));
      P('第一个子节点的 HTML：');
      P(String(best.children[0].outerHTML || '').slice(0, 1200));
      P('父节点 <' + best.parentElement.tagName.toLowerCase() + idOf(best.parentElement) +
        clsOf(best.parentElement) + '> 子节点=' + best.parentElement.children.length);
    } else {
      P('没找到「≥4 个带图的直接子节点」的容器 —— 说明列表可能不在 #tg-user-tinygrail 里，或卡片没图。');
    }

    var s = L.join('\n');
    try { window.__tgsDump = s; } catch (e) { }
    console.log('%c[DOM 快照] 把下面整段复制发我', 'color:#0a0;font-weight:bold', '\n' + s);
    return s;
  };
})();
